Today `site_or_404` (`:209-225`) raises 409 `AWAITING_EVIDENCE_MIGRATION` for an imported Site via `backend/assetops_backend/sites/cutover.py:114-119`; the UI shows `AwaitingEvidenceMigration` (`frontend/src/sites/SiteOverview.tsx:93-95`). Only legacy Sites reach the file-backed `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/overview.py:575-640` model. `frontend/src/sites/siteOverviewClient.ts:138-164,197-263` validates that old shape. **New:** make this existing handler delegate imported Sites to the versioned S5 read service and keep its legacy branch; do not register another route at this path, which the first included evidence router would shadow. Do not reuse the old Findings array as a Site index.
- Gate and scope composition (**new**): in `main.py`, inject into the existing evidence router an S5 service, `registry.ledger_scope` (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/cutover.py:107-111`), the same S2-02 `ImportedEvidenceReads.scope` cutover check (`backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:229-245`), the same `performance_gate` (`backend/assetops_backend/main.py:329-340`), and the Finding `Access` hook/`access_basis` convention (`backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:36-40,63-77`). Apply `Access` before tenant/Site discovery; return its 401/403, use `ledger_scope` for the exact tenant/Site IDs, and expose the configured `access_basis` in the S5 response. For imported Sites, the S2 comparison must pass before the Overview serves accepted context, timeline, or evidence links: when absent/failed return the existing 409 `AWAITING_EVIDENCE_MIGRATION` for the whole imported Overview. The Performance gate controls `freshness.source_open`, selected S3 objective/status and current Finding/money claims; when closed, keep only explicitly pinned audit material and typed unavailable/closed sections, with no CURRENT assessment or amount. When both gates open, the selected S3/accepted-evidence sections may be current if their own fences pass; money still requires its separate gates. A closed Performance gate does not bypass the S2 comparison. Test comparison absent, Performance closed with comparison passed, and both open on the same route, including tenant/Site and access denials.
- `SiteWindowSummary` and `site_window_projection` exist only in design (`frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:68`); no runtime table/class exists. Choose an **equivalent scoped read over committed public records**; no new table/migration. Create the S5 read service/DTO (**new**) and reuse `backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/composition.py:45-56` `build_upstream_currentness` for one `UpstreamCurrentness` instance per process, disposed at shutdown. `backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/upstream.py:75-82,87-202` provides DB time, S3 day, match, Finding/consequence work and schedule eligibility. Its consequence read passes `source_open=True` at line 140 on the LR-01 head: S5 must apply the real Performance source gate before any current claim. Fence the selected sequence, accepted-source basis and work before/after composition; if they move, return stale/pending, never mixed CURRENT. Derived reads need no write-side invalidation.
- LR-01 **(unmerged, stacked; citations checked at `task/LR-01-late-reading-series-and-money-limit` head)** adds `backend/assetops_backend/financials/money_eligibility.py:12-23,39-44,170-186` (`MoneyEligibility.for_finding`; `ELIGIBLE`, `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, `PENDING_REASSESSMENT`). `backend/assetops_backend/financials/consequence_read.py:214-239` exposes the gate and gates `current_amount_shown`; `backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/upstream.py:149-161,189-200` includes the same result per Finding. Consume it; do not build a UI-only predicate. `ELIGIBLE` alone is never amount authority. LR-01 owns source-time attestation, overlap, six-hour requeue and explicit-epoch limits; S5 shows the selected basis/limit and preserves old exact versions.
- Existing source gate pattern: `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:79-85,112-128,246-277` (closed source permits pinned audit material, no unpinned current claim). Reuse it in S5 (**new integration**). Exact destinations already exist: dispatch day `backend/assetops_backend/performance_api.py:88`, Finding and financial consequence `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:112,246,284`, assumption revision `backend/assetops_backend/financial_read_api.py:88`, evidence record `backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:158`. UI links must carry exact revision IDs; add a route only where an exact destination is absent (**new**).

## Read contract (all DTO fields **new** unless the seam above names them)

`GET /api/sites/{site_code}/overview?date=YYYY-MM-DD` selects one UTC day. With no date or range, imported Sites default to the DB clock's current UTC day and return that day in `window.utc_date`; the UI writes it to the URL for reload. Accept existing `from`/`to` only when exactly one UTC day; reject partial, conflicting or non-UTC selection with typed 422. For legacy Sites, translate `date=` into the same midnight `from`/`to` pair and retain the existing file-backed DTO; with no selection retain its existing `LATEST_RELEASE` default. Return versioned `SiteWindowSummary` for imported Sites:

| Field | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `schema_version, site_id, site_code, access_basis, window {start,end,utc_date}, built_at, db_now` | Exact Site/day, configured development access basis and DB-time basis. |
| `freshness {state,stage,reasons,errors,source_open,source_reason}` | `UNASSESSED` (UI Unknown), `PENDING`, `STALE_PROJECTION`, `FAILED`, `CURRENT`; carry typed S3/schedule/work reasons. Closed source cannot be CURRENT. `S3_UNASSESSED` is a gap, never success. |
| `watermarks {accepted_source,analytic_selection_seq,input_sha256,configuration_sha256,match_pass_id,matched_through_selection_seq}, model_version` | Accepted ledger/source and exact analytic/match basis. `accepted_source` is a **new** deterministic fingerprint and count of accepted ledger IDs intersecting the selected day plus S3's six-hour query bracket, ordered by stable ID; compare it before/after read. Missing ledger access gives null plus reason and blocks CURRENT. Never use heartbeat/disposition time as the fence. |
| `assessment {run_id,window_assessment_revision_id,status,claim_level,limitations,coverage,dispatch_intervals,links}` | One selected S3 day; unassessed objective is Unknown. Timeline cites the selected assessment and accepted evidence; do not recalculate dispatch. |
| `context {series,coverage,gaps,evidence_links}` | Accepted measurements with time/unit/signal/evidence ID and quality limit. Missing/conflicting inputs give null and a reason. |
| `money {state,amount_low,amount_high,currency,model_version,finding_id,finding_revision_id,consequence_revision_id,eligibility,quantity_state,price_state,limitation,demonstration,links}` | One Finding only. Amount low is zero and upper is bounded only for exact-head, same FindingRevision/window/basis `CURRENT PRICED`, open source, upstream CURRENT and shared `ELIGIBLE`. Both amount fields use the outward-rounded `display` values from that same consequence revision; its unrounded `amount` remains exact drill-down material. `MINOR_UNIT_UNKNOWN` gives no displayed amount. Otherwise both fields are null with limiting-state links. |
| `known_finding {id,revision_id,selection_rule,link}, finding_list {state:"UNAVAILABLE",reason}` | A known selected Finding can link directly; no list rows/count/empty claim. |

Architect-ruled Finding choice (`D-2026-10-15-s501-finding-choice`): discover the latest recorded FindingRevision of each tenant/Site-scoped canonical Finding in the same fenced S3/S4 basis. Include only episodes with half-open intersection `episode_start < day_end and episode_end > day_start`; an episode ending exactly at `00:00Z` on the selected day is excluded. The S4 `findings_over` bracket read (`backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_finding_store.py:418-431`) uses a wider bracket and a closed lower-end test, so explicitly re-filter and exclude IDs superseded by an S4 merge. Do not filter by lifecycle, consequence state, price, amount or money eligibility. Choose greatest `episode_start`, then lexicographically smallest stable Finding UUID. Return the rule, Finding ID and exact revision ID. If several intersect, say the card covers this Finding only. Keep that pinned Finding and exact links through pending, stale, withheld, unpriced or other unavailable money. A changed, consistently selected S4 episode basis may change the choice; an incomplete or moving basis retains the previous pinned choice as stale/pending without current amount. `ABSENT` is allowed only after complete, current scoped discovery finds no intersecting canonical Finding.

S5 no-amount states (**new DTO vocabulary**): `ABSENT`, `PENDING_REASSESSMENT`, `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, `SOURCE_CLOSED`, `UPSTREAM_UNASSESSED`, `UPSTREAM_PENDING`, `UPSTREAM_STALE`, `UPSTREAM_FAILED`, `SUPERSEDED`, `CONSEQUENCE_PENDING`, `CONSEQUENCE_STALE`, `QUANTITY_UNAVAILABLE`, `UNPRICED`, `MINOR_UNIT_UNKNOWN`, `MIXED_OR_UNKNOWN_SOURCE`. Preserve underlying LR eligibility, S4 currentness/reasons, work IDs and historical links. Precedence: source closed; upstream failed/stale/pending/unassessed (including `S3_UNASSESSED`); LR pending then withheld; superseded; consequence pending/stale; quantity unavailable; unpriced; unknown currency minor unit; mixed/unknown source; only then priced. `MIXED_OR_UNKNOWN_SOURCE` is produced when `demonstration_case` returns the unverified label because its fixture specification cannot be read (`backend/assetops_backend/demonstration_label.py:60-69`); if no other trusted provenance resolves that case, show no amount. A historical `CURRENT PRICED` row remains audit-only after series ambiguity. Reuse `backend/assetops_backend/demonstration_label.py:60-76` and Finding bridge `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:275,306` for authored P01. Site/day matching is a bounded fixture label, **not** stored authorship. Label the `fuel-cost-1` model-conditioned potential fuel-cost exposure, zero lower bound and assumption/evidence lineage; never savings or a physical maximum.

The S5 amount read must use the consequence revision whose ID equals the selected FindingRevision's seam `head_consequence_revision_id` (`backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/upstream.py:155-161` on LR-01). If a later exact read observes a different head, or its FindingRevision/window/basis changed, fail closed as stale/pending and retry on a new request; never combine an old seam verdict with a newer amount.

## Read contract (route, DTO and cursor are new)

Add `GET /api/sites/{site_code}/finding-index?domain=mini-grid-dispatch&from=<UTC>&to=<UTC>&limit=<bounded>&cursor=<opaque>`. `mini-grid-dispatch` is a **new API domain token**: resolve it to stored `finding.domain='mini_grid' AND finding.analytic_id='dispatch'`, the S3 `dispatch` discovery basis, and the S4 matcher `MATCHER_VERSION` on that basis (`analytics/finding_episode.py:78,76` on the stacked base; `:68` for `DOMAIN` on main; `analytics/dispatch.py:27`; `analytics/assessment_worker.py:51`). Resolve tenant/Site via Finding access before discovery: established 401/403/404 and no foreign count. Require the explicit supported domain and UTC half-open `[from,to)`, `from < to`, maximum 31 UTC days; any other domain or invalid input receives typed 422, with no status, rows or count, never `CURRENT` zero. Echo the requested and resolved domain/analytic, Site, UTC window and display timezone. The UI persists Site/domain/dates in its URL; date change makes a new read. Server bounds page size.

Return `schema_version`, Site/domain/window, display timezone/access basis, selection token, `knowledge_at`, `built_at`, typed status/reasons, coverage/limitations, watermarks, rows, `total_count` and `next_cursor`. `total_count` is null unless status is `CURRENT`. Watermarks contain accepted-source marker, S3 selected assessment/run/input/configuration and analytic selection, S4 matcher/version/pass/selection. Each row has stable `(tenant_id,site_id,finding_id)` key, canonical Finding UUID, exact `finding_revision_id`, episode bounds, lifecycle/transition, selected assessment/run/model/cohort, cited accepted-evidence/configuration IDs, limitations, relation/history links and `/sites/{site_code}/findings/{finding_id}?revision_id={revision_id}`. Exact links survive reload and newer revisions; lineage comes from the selected immutable revision.

Use one transactionally consistent read (no published projection in S4-04); `knowledge_at` is a selection cutoff, not arbitrary commit-ordered replay. **Complete discovery** starts with every UTC-day analytic window intersecting `[from,to)` and repeatedly inspects adjacent UTC days reachable across the current edge through an actual S4 20-minute continuity bridge on one fenced accepted-evidence/configuration/model basis, until a fixed point. This can extend beyond one neighbour on either side. Every inspected day needs S3 selection and canonical S4 match through its current selection seq; record each day and its S3/S4 selection seq in the selection token, including days with no row. Cap discovery at 62 distinct UTC-day windows total, including requested days. If the next reachable day exceeds the cap, return 409 `INDEX_CONTINUITY_LIMIT` with a reason and no rows, count or empty claim; this service cap does not prove continuity ended. Fence before/after read. Moving selection, affected dirty/leased/failed work, watermark mismatch, or late evidence/configuration/model change blocks `CURRENT`. A source-coverage limitation can coexist with a current assessment of available evidence, but cannot assert physical completeness.

`PENDING` means no usable completed selection; `UNASSESSED` no completed discovery basis; `STALE_PROJECTION` and `FAILED` may expose rows only from a durable prior selected snapshot holding exact canonical Finding/FindingRevision IDs, sort order and response basis. Recheck scope and label such rows/count historical, never current. S4-04 builds **no durable snapshot**, migration or new writer. Consequently stale/failed reads return no rows and null `total_count`; use `PENDING` or `UNASSESSED` when no usable completed selection exists, and `FAILED` may report the failure without historical rows. Never reconstruct stale rows from newer heads. None authorizes current count, empty/Healthy assertion or swapping stale links for a new head. A closed S3 Performance source gate returns 409 `PERFORMANCE_CUTOVER_CLOSED` with its reason and no rows/count/empty claim; exact pinned detail remains a separate audit route. Consume S4-05's reviewed stored gate form when merged.

One row per canonical Finding; merged-away IDs remain relationship links. Extension changes its revision; separated recurrence gets another Finding ID and link. Include closed and unconfirmed episodes. Intersect iff `episode_start < to && episode_end > from`; boundary touch is excluded. Order selected `episode_start` descending, then stable Finding UUID ascending; never by money, lifecycle or arrival. Cursor binds tenant/Site/domain/window, basis/selection token and last sort key. A well-formed cursor whose bound scope or fenced basis changed returns 409 `INDEX_BASIS_CHANGED` with a machine-readable reason and instruction to request page one without a cursor; return no rows, count or replacement cursor. A malformed cursor returns typed 422. Pages cannot splice revision sets. No priority tuple until a producer is reviewed.

Optional amount cell starts **unavailable**. On the stacked S5-01/LR-01 baseline, LR-01 `money_eligibility` is reviewed but S4-05 stored accepted-ID provenance is unmerged: show no current amount. After **both** reviewed contracts enter the baseline, separate review may allow amount only for the exact selected FindingRevision/window/basis and consequence head, open source, current list/consequence, eligible series, priced quantity/price and known source/display unit. Withheld/pending, mixed/unknown source, stale, wrong revision/window, unpriced or closed source means no current amount; old values use explicit audit links. No sum, savings inference or unpriced-as-zero. Money does not gate row/empty delivery.

## Phases, focused tests and review

| Phase | Reviewable result and proof |

# Backend testing guide

How to run the backend suite, when to run all of it, and how to prove a full
run was real. Database setup itself is in [README-db.md](README-db.md).

## When to run what

- **While building a phase:** run the focused files for the area you changed,
  plus adjacent integration tests. When an interface crosses into the host or
  gateway, also run those suites.
- **At the end of an ordinary slice:** one real-Postgres full backend suite,
  on the slice's final revision, with the JUnit zero-skip check below. Cite it
  in the review packet.
- **Reviewers** run the focused files relevant to their findings and cite the
  implementer's same-revision full evidence. A reviewer reruns the full suite
  only for a finding that needs it.
- **One full suite at a time on this machine.** Concurrent full runs were not
  attempted and are not proven safe; a full run is disk-bound, so a second one
  (or another Postgres-heavy agent) slows both and makes timing meaningless.
- OPS-01's baseline and comparison runs are the one documented exception to
  "one full suite per slice".
- `tools/check-all.ps1` is a separate required guard command; it does not run
  pytest, and pytest does not replace it.

## The test database

`ASSETOPS_TEST_DATABASE_URL` names a base database whose name ends in `_test`
(Compose creates `assetops_test`; agents may use a private one such as
`assetops_<task>_test`). Without it every Postgres module is reported as
**skipped**, and such a run is not valid full-suite proof.

The base database is used only as an administrative connection. At
configuration time `tests/conftest.py` calls `pg_harness.route()`, which
rewrites `ASSETOPS_TEST_DATABASE_URL` to a run-owned name before any test module
is imported:

```
<base without _test>_pt<run>_<tag>_test     tag = main (serial), gw0, gw1... (xdist workers), tpl (template)
```

On first use the harness migrates the template to head once, then every
Postgres module's `engine` fixture drops the run's worker database `WITH
(FORCE)` and clones it from the template (`CREATE DATABASE ... TEMPLATE`), and
asserts the clone is at the Alembic head. Each module therefore still starts on
a fresh, fully migrated schema; migration up/downgrade tests still run in place
on that module's clone. `store` fixtures yield and dispose their engine.

Between tests the S1 `store` fixture empties the same tables
`TRUNCATE foundation_revision, source, gateway, asset, site_import, site, tenant
CASCADE` would, but with `DELETE` under `session_replication_role = replica`
(`pg_harness.empty_tables`). TRUNCATE rebuilds every index and on PostgreSQL 16
each rebuild forces a data-file sync, which Docker Desktop makes slow and
erratic (measured 1.4 s quiet, 22-25 s under disk pressure, per test; DELETE
0.14 s). `test_pg_harness.py` proves the DELETE set equals the tables TRUNCATE
CASCADE reports. `ASSETOPS_TEST_CLEANUP=truncate` restores TRUNCATE.

- In parallel (`-n 2`) the xdist controller runs no tests. It makes the run
  id and passes it to each worker; each worker routes itself to
  `..._pt<run>_gw<N>_test` before it imports a test module. The first worker
  that needs the template builds it under an advisory lock and the others reuse
  it. Workers drop only their own database; the controller drops the template
  and anything else the run left (a crashed worker's database) at the end.
  Under `--dist loadfile` every module stays on one worker, so module-scoped
  fixtures behave as serially. With `--no-loadscope-reorder` modules are handed
  out in collection order, and workers move the two long S4 Finding modules
  (`conftest.START_FIRST`) to the front so they start first; serial order is
  unchanged.
- Parametrize IDs must not be random: xdist refuses to run when workers collect
  different node IDs (`Different tests were collected between gw0 and gw1`).
- Nothing is provisioned for `--collect-only`, for a selection without Postgres
  modules, or with the URL unset.
- At the end of the session the run drops its own databases. A killed run's
  databases are swept by the next run with the same base: it drops
  `<base>_pt*` databases only when their run holds no live session (each run
  keeps one lease session whose `application_name` is
  `assetops-test-run:<run>`).
- `ASSETOPS_TEST_TEMPLATE=0` turns routing off and restores the old behaviour
  (drop `public` and migrate in place per module). Use it only if cloning is
  unavailable; it is serial-only and slower.
- Measurement knobs, off by default: `ASSETOPS_TEST_CLONE_STRATEGY`
  (`WAL_LOG` or `FILE_COPY`) and `ASSETOPS_TEST_SYNCHRONOUS_COMMIT` (applied with
  `ALTER DATABASE` to run-owned databases only). Server-wide settings are never
  changed by the tests.

## Commands (PowerShell, from the repository root)

```powershell
$env:ASSETOPS_TEST_DATABASE_URL = "postgresql+psycopg://assetops:assetops-local-dev@127.0.0.1:55432/assetops_test"
$py = ".venv\Scripts\python.exe"

# S1 Foundation and bindings
& $py -m pytest backend/tests/test_s1_foundation_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s1_bindings_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s1_cutover.py backend/tests/test_s1_bindings.py

# S2 ingestion and evidence
& $py -m pytest backend/tests/test_s2_evidence_ledger_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s2_gateway_access_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s2_site_evidence_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s2_registered_basis_postgres.py

# S3 dispatch
& $py -m pytest backend/tests/test_s3_dispatch_assessment_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s3_dispatch_read_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s3_demo_configuration.py

# S4 Finding and money
& $py -m pytest backend/tests/test_s4_finding_store_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_finding_read_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_finding_index_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_finding_evidence_history_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_assumptions_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_consequence_store_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_consequence_recompute_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_evidence_consequence_work_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_evidence_drain_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_power_demo_case_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_schedule_tick_postgres.py backend/tests/test_s4_schedule_health_postgres.py

# Complete suite - the normal full command (two workers), with JUnit for the zero-skip proof
& $py -m pytest backend/tests -n 2 --dist loadfile --no-loadscope-reorder -q --durations=30 --junitxml=.pytest-junit.xml

# Complete suite, serial (fallback; also what a serial re-check of one file uses)
& $py -m pytest backend/tests -q --durations=30 --junitxml=.pytest-junit.xml
```

### Before a long full run

Start the keep-awake helper in a separate window (or as a background job). It
asks Windows not to idle-sleep while any pytest process runs, changes no power
settings, and exits after 20 idle minutes. It also holds the display on: on a Modern
Standby laptop the display idle timeout otherwise enters standby (System event
506, "Reason: Idle Timeout") even with the system-required flag, and a run that
crosses standby loses wall time it never computed (main-tick-cost: the four-Site
tick-cost test exhausted its 240 s tick budget and visited 3 Sites; the full
suite took 47 minutes). Check with
`Get-WinEvent -FilterHashtable @{LogName='System';Id=506,507}` before reading a
slow or budget-bound failure as a code regression. A machine that sleeps mid-run turns
a 40-minute suite into hours (seen in OPS-01: one run reached 34% in 4 h).

```powershell
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\keep-awake-during-tests.ps1
```

Cap every full run at twice its expected wall time (see Timing below). A run
past the cap is reported as a hang, with the node ID it is stuck on (from
`pg_stat_activity` and the pytest log), not waited on.

### Zero-skip proof (from JUnit, not the summary line)

```powershell
[xml]$j = Get-Content .pytest-junit.xml
$cases = $j.SelectNodes("//testcase")
$skipped = $j.SelectNodes("//testcase[skipped]")
$failed = $j.SelectNodes("//testcase[failure or error]")
"cases=$($cases.Count) skipped=$($skipped.Count) failed=$($failed.Count)"
$skipped | ForEach-Object { "$($_.classname)::$($_.name) - $($_.skipped.message)" }
```

A full run is valid proof only when `skipped` is 0 and `failed` is 0. A skip
whose message names `ASSETOPS_TEST_DATABASE_URL` means the database was unset.

## Timing

Measured on this Windows host (Docker Desktop Postgres 16), serial, full
suite. Run-to-run variance is large (disk sync inside Docker Desktop), so
compare only back-to-back runs in a quiet window.

| Revision | Runs (wall) | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Before OPS-01 (in-place migrate per module, TRUNCATE per test) | 80.3 min, 41.4 min; peak 91 sessions | 2 timing-sensitive tick failures in the 80-min run; a third attempt reached 34% in 4 h while the machine slept |
| Serial template clone + DELETE cleanup (OPS-01 phase 1) | 30.8 min (back-to-back with the 41.4-min baseline); peak 22 sessions | template clone without DELETE cleanup: 41.4 min, one 23-min disk stall in a tick test |
| `-n 2 --dist loadfile` (OPS-01 phase 2, normal command) | 7.7 min, 8.4 min; peak 35 and 32 sessions | 2128 passed, 0 skipped, 0 failed both times; node IDs identical to serial collection |
| `-n 3 --dist loadfile` (OPS-01 phase 2, one run) | 6.5 min; peak 41 sessions | 2128 passed; not repeated, so not the default |

Expected `-n 2` full run: about 8-10 min; cap at 20 min. Expected serial full
run: about 30-45 min; cap at 90 min. Use `-n 2`, never `-n auto`: the suite is
disk-bound inside Docker Desktop, and each worker adds about 10-20 sessions
against `max_connections = 100`.

If a timing-sensitive test (S4 tick hung-stage and claims replay, health skew
and overlap race, drain 30 s deadline, work enqueue-lock poll) fails under
`-n 2`, do not retry it: record the node ID and its duration, rerun that file
serially as a separate invocation, and report both runs' JUnit counts.
"""S5-01 phase 2: the repeatable owner walkthrough for the selected-window Site Overview (tasks/S5-01-site-overview.md,

    prepare   build the tree and database, seed every day, pass the S2 Evidence and Performance comparisons, tick
              the schedule and verify every day through the served Overview; STOPS (exit 1) instead of promising
              an amount that is not there
    serve     re-run the Performance comparison (unless the gate is closed) and serve the API on 8925 (foreground)
    attest    LR-01's SYSTEM-only labelled simulated clock attestation for P02 (no tick)
    tick      one explicit schedule pass in the `default` environment the API reads (no OS schedule installed),
              then the Performance comparison
    stop-schedule   mark the private schedule stopped and age its heartbeat and Site head past the production
                    thresholds, so the served Overview reads SCHEDULE_STOPPED at once
    start-schedule  clear the stop and run one schedule pass, restoring private observation
    gate --close | gate --open   move the Performance comparison reports aside / back (the source gate)
    drop      drop the database and remove the work directory

The schedule heartbeat uses the production thresholds (`scheduling/health.py`: 300 s environment, 900 s Site).
With no OS schedule installed, the served Overview reads SCHEDULE_STOPPED (no amount) once five minutes pass after
the last `tick`: run `tick` again before viewing an amount.
"""

import s402c_late_power_walkthrough as lr  # noqa: E402

p, s4, s2 = lr.p, lr.s4, lr.s2

FAIL_STAGE = r'''
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from assetops_backend.analytics.adapters.postgres_assessment_store import PostgresAssessmentStore
from assetops_backend.analytics.assessment_inputs import Window
from assetops_backend.analytics.assessment_worker import DispatchAssessmentWorker
from assetops_backend.evidence.composition import build_evidence_ledger

url, failed_day = sys.argv[1:3]
tenant, site = build_evidence_ledger(url).site_scope("demo", "KOBO-001")
store = PostgresAssessmentStore(url)
now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
start = datetime.fromisoformat(failed_day + "T00:00:00+00:00")

class StagedFailure:
    """Walkthrough-only: a model that always fails, scoped to one day, to show a FAILED day (S3 walkthrough)."""
    ANALYTIC_ID, MODEL_VERSION = "dispatch", "dispatch-3.1"

    @staticmethod
    def assess(_inputs):
        raise RuntimeError("walkthrough staged failure: the assessment could not be computed")

store.enqueue(tenant, site, "dispatch", Window(start, start + timedelta(days=1)), now)
for attempt in range(3):
    DispatchAssessmentWorker(store, model=StagedFailure, clock=lambda: now + timedelta(seconds=attempt),
                             backoff=timedelta(0)).run_pending(reconcile=False)
print("staged: FAILED", failed_day)
'''


def say(text: str) -> None:
    s4.say(text)


def step(text: str) -> None:
    s4.step(text)


def pg_url(db: str) -> str:
    return (f"postgresql://assetops:{os.environ.get('ASSETOPS_PG_PASSWORD', 'assetops-local-dev')}"
            f"@127.0.0.1:{os.environ.get('ASSETOPS_PG_PORT', '55432')}/{db}")


def get_json(url: str, timeout: float = 30) -> tuple[int, Any]:
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=timeout) as response:
            return response.status, json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8"))
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
        try:
            return error.code, json.loads(error.read().decode("utf-8"))
        except ValueError:
            return error.code, None


def overview(api_port: int, day: str, pin: tuple[str, str] | None = None) -> tuple[int, Any]:
    query = f"date={day}" + (f"&finding_id={pin[0]}&finding_revision_id={pin[1]}" if pin else "")
    return get_json(f"http://127.0.0.1:{api_port}/api/sites/{SITE}/overview?{query}")


def summary_line(day: str, status: int, body: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if status != 200 or not isinstance(body, dict) or "money" not in body:
        return {"day": day, "http": status, "body": body}
    m, k = body["money"], body.get("known_finding") or {}
    return {"day": day, "http": status, "freshness": body["freshness"]["state"],
            "reasons": body["freshness"]["reasons"], "source_open": body["freshness"]["source_open"],
            "assessment": body["assessment"]["state"], "money": m["state"],
            "gate": (m.get("eligibility") or {}).get("state"), "amount": [m["amount_low"], m["amount_high"],
                                                                           m["currency"]],
            "demonstration": (m.get("demonstration") or {}).get("case_id"), "finding_id": k.get("id"),
            "finding_revision_id": k.get("revision_id"), "consequence_revision_id": m.get("consequence_revision_id"),
            "accepted_source": body["watermarks"]["accepted_source"],
            "analytic_selection_seq": body["watermarks"]["analytic_selection_seq"],
            "match_pass_id": body["watermarks"]["match_pass_id"], "previous_pin": body.get("previous_pin")}


class S5Walk(lr.Lr01Walk):
    """Every producer in one database; the LR-01 walk supplies P02 and the SYSTEM price."""

    def __init__(self, args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
        super().__init__(args)
        self.p01_spec = json.loads((s2.REPO / p.POWER_SPEC_PATH).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.price_done: Any = None
        self.out.update(s501=True, days=DAYS)

    def backend(self, *argv: str, check: bool = True):
        # The LR-01 walk publishes SYSTEM price v1 as its step 0; this walk publishes it first (before P01), so the
        # second publication is the first one's result, not a duplicate assumption revision.
        if "assetops_backend.publish_assumption" in argv and self.price_done is not None:
            return self.price_done
        done = super().backend(*argv, check=check)
        if "assetops_backend.publish_assumption" in argv:
            self.price_done = done
        return done

    def seed_p01(self) -> None:
        case = self.p01_spec["cases"][0]
        step(f"P01. {case['case_id']} ({case['day']}, {self.p01_spec['label']})")
        late_spec = self.power_spec
        self.power_spec = self.p01_spec
        try:
            self.run_power_case(case)
        finally:
            self.power_spec = late_spec
        self.drain("P01 consequence")

    def stage_failed_day(self) -> None:
        step(f"FAILED day {DAYS['failed']}: a scoped failing assessment (walkthrough-only model)")
        done = self.backend("-c", FAIL_STAGE, self.db_url, DAYS["failed"])
        say("  " + done.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1])
"""S4-04 phase 2: the repeatable owner walkthrough for the Site Finding index (tasks/S4-04-site-finding-index.md,
"Reproducible owner walkthrough").

It composes the existing Finding-case producer (`tools/s4_finding_cases.py`, labelled authenticated fixture
batches over TLS, the S3 worker and the S4 matcher) in ONE private database and work directory, and adds:

- an independent **assessed empty** day (2026-10-25): a delivered non-candidate block, assessed and matched, so
  the index reads CURRENT with zero rows and a complete S3/S4 watermark;
- the **positive** day (2026-10-15, the F06 day), whose canonical Findings and exact revisions drive order, cursor
  pages (page size 2) and the S5-01 card parity;
- a **dirty/stale** day (2026-10-27): assessed and matched, then late evidence is delivered and not assessed;
- a **failed** day (2026-11-05, apart from the dirty day so its failed job is not read for it): the scoped
  failing-job pattern of `tools/s5_overview_walkthrough.py`;
- an **unassessed** day (2026-10-30): nothing is delivered for it.

Commands (database `assetops_s404_owner*` and work directory `var/s404_owner*` only; API 8934, web 5434):

    prepare        build the tree and database, seed every fixture, run the Performance comparison (source gate),
                   and verify every window through the index read; writes <work>/s404-manifest.json; exits 1 when
                   the assessed-empty completeness or any expected status cannot be proved
    serve          serve the API on 8934 (foreground) and print the frontend command and owner URLs; installs no
                   schedule
    late           deliver a late Candidate block on the positive day (2026-10-15 16:00-16:40), not assessed
    settle         tick S3 assessment (`assess_dispatch process-jobs`) and S4 matching (`match-findings`) to
                   completion, then re-run the Performance comparison
    gate --close | gate --open   move the Performance comparison reports aside / back (the source gate)
    drop           drop the database and remove the work directory

Run from the repository root with the repository venv (`.venv`). `var/sites/kobo-001.yaml` must exist.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.parse
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

DOMAIN = "mini-grid-dispatch"

        "unassessed": "2026-10-30"}
CANDIDATE = {"pv": "40", "dis": "20", "soc": "80", "stop_before": True, "stop_after": True}

LATE_BLOCK = ("2026-10-15T16:00:00Z", "2026-10-15T16:40:00Z")
say, step = s2.say, s2.step


def window(day: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    from datetime import date, timedelta

    nxt = date.fromisoformat(day) + timedelta(days=1)
    return f"{day}T00:00:00Z", f"{nxt.isoformat()}T00:00:00Z"


def block(start: str, end: str, kind: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"from": start, "to": end, **kind}


def fixture(case_id: str, day: str, steps: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"case_id": case_id, "label": f"S4-04 walkthrough {case_id}", "day": day, "steps": steps}


def index_url(api_port: int, frm: str, to: str, **extra: str) -> str:
    query = urllib.parse.urlencode({"domain": DOMAIN, "from": frm, "to": to, **extra})
    return f"http://127.0.0.1:{api_port}/api/sites/{SITE}/finding-index?{query}"


def get_json(url: str, timeout: float = 60) -> tuple[int, Any]:
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=timeout) as response:
            return response.status, json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8"))
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
        return error.code, json.loads(error.read().decode("utf-8") or "null")


def summary(status: int, body: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if status != 200:
        detail = (body or {}).get("detail") if isinstance(body, dict) else body
        return {"http": status, "code": detail.get("code") if isinstance(detail, dict) else None,
                "reason": detail.get("reason") if isinstance(detail, dict) else None}
    return {"http": 200, "status": body["status"], "total_count": body["total_count"],
            "rows": [[r["finding_id"], r["finding_revision_id"], r["episode"]["start"]] for r in body["rows"]],
            "next_cursor": bool(body["next_cursor"]), "selection_token": body["selection_token"],
            "days": body["discovery"]["days"], "reasons": body["status_reasons"][:6]}


class IndexWalk(s4.Cases):
    """The S4 Finding-case producer in a private database, plus the S4-04 index fixtures."""

    def __init__(self, args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
        super().__init__(args)
        self.out: dict[str, Any] = {"database": args.db, "days": DAYS, "checks": []}

    def deliver_case(self, case_id: str, day: str, blocks: list[dict[str, Any]], *, assess: bool = True,
                     match: bool = True, decided: str = "2026-10-31T08:00:00Z") -> None:
        steps: list[dict[str, Any]] = [{"do": "deliver", "decided": decided, "blocks": blocks}]
        if assess:
            steps.append({"do": "assess"})
        if match:
            steps.append({"do": "match", "expect": [], "collateral": True})
        self.run_case(fixture(case_id, day, steps))

    def fixtures(self) -> None:
        for n, case in enumerate(self.spec["cases"], start=4):
            step(f"{n}. {case['case_id']} {case['label']} ({case['day']})")
            self.run_case(case)
        step(f"EMPTY {DAYS['empty']}: one non-candidate block, assessed and matched (assessed zero rows)")
        self.deliver_case("E1", DAYS["empty"], [block(f"{DAYS['empty']}T10:00:00Z", f"{DAYS['empty']}T10:30:00Z",
                                                      NO_CANDIDATE)])
        step(f"DIRTY {DAYS['dirty']}: assessed and matched, then late evidence left unassessed")
        self.deliver_case("D1", DAYS["dirty"], [block(f"{DAYS['dirty']}T10:00:00Z", f"{DAYS['dirty']}T10:30:00Z",
                                                      NO_CANDIDATE)])
        step(f"FAILED {DAYS['failed']}: delivered, then a scoped failing assessment (walkthrough-only model)")
        self.deliver_case("X1", DAYS["failed"], [block(f"{DAYS['failed']}T10:00:00Z",
                                                       f"{DAYS['failed']}T10:30:00Z", NO_CANDIDATE)],
                          assess=False, match=False)
        import s5_overview_walkthrough as s5

        done = self.py("-c", s5.FAIL_STAGE, self.db_url, DAYS["failed"], cwd=self.tree / "backend")
        say("  " + done.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1])
        self.deliver_case("D2", DAYS["dirty"], [block(f"{DAYS['dirty']}T12:00:00Z", f"{DAYS['dirty']}T12:30:00Z",
                                                      NO_CANDIDATE)], assess=False, match=False,
                          decided="2026-11-01T08:00:00Z")
        say(f"UNASSESSED {DAYS['unassessed']}: nothing delivered")

    def run_all(self) -> None:
        self.setup()
        try:
            self.prepare()
            self.fixtures()
        finally:
            step("Stopping every process this script started")
            self.stop_all()

def tree_py(work: Path, db: str, *argv: str, check: bool = True) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    import s402c_late_power_walkthrough as lr

    return lr.tree_py(work, db, *argv, check=check)


def comparison(work: Path, db: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    live = work / "tree" / GATE_DIR
    if live.with_name(live.name + ".closed").exists():
        say("  Performance gate is closed (gate --close): the comparison is not re-run.")
        return {"open": False, "closed_by_walkthrough": True}
    done = tree_py(work, db, "-m", "assetops_backend.performance_migration", "run", "--tenant", "demo",
                   "--site", SITE, check=False)
    say(f"  Performance comparison (source gate): exit {done.returncode} -> "
        f"{'OPEN' if done.returncode == 0 else 'CLOSED'}")
    if done.returncode:
        say("  " + (done.stdout or done.stderr)[-600:].replace("\n", "\n  "))

4149:Decision: `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`. Under the owner-absence work order item 9, resolve `A-finding-index` for a tenant/Site/domain and selected half-open UTC window of at most 31 days. The W1 read selects the latest immutable S4-01 FindingRevision of each canonical Finding from one consistent S3/S4 basis, includes every intersecting lifecycle state, and uses strict half-open overlap. Its stable row key is `(tenant_id, site_id, finding_id)`; default order is episode start descending, then Finding UUID ascending. Return exact revision/detail and assessment/evidence lineage, scoped accepted-source and analytic/matcher watermarks, selection token, built time, coverage and typed currentness. Complete discovery requires every intersected UTC-day analytic window and neighbours reachable through 20-minute episode continuity matched through each current selection seq. Only complete current scoped discovery under an open S3 Performance cutover gate may state zero Findings; pending, stale, failed and unassessed reads remain visibly Unknown or historical. A closed gate refuses the unpinned index with HTTP 409 `PERFORMANCE_CUTOVER_CLOSED` and its reason, with neither current rows nor a no-Findings claim, as on Finding routes; S4-05 stored provenance joins that gate once merged. A changed basis invalidates the bound page cursor. S4-04 adds a new index read without changing the consumed `findings_over` helper, and must independently prove a watermarked assessed zero-row fixture, a closed-gate 409 variant, a persisted positive-row fixture, exact links after reload, invalidation, Site isolation, first-row equality with the S5-01 card Finding on the same basis, and narrow/desktop UI. Index rows do not depend on money; optional current consequence display consumes LR-01 money eligibility and S4-05 exact-revision source provenance after their reviewed branches enter the build baseline, plus the existing exact-head, quantity, price and currentness gates. Mixed/unknown source and withheld/pending eligibility expose no current amount. S5-02 reuses the Site/window rows for both `mini:3` variants after S4-04 and S5-01 review; S4-03, S5-01's other panels and S3 dispatch do not wait on the index. Full contract and implementation handoff: `.agent/architect-A-finding-index-answers.md`. This decision grants no owner UI copy or walkthrough acceptance and does not implement a global or cross-domain index.
4151:Decision: `D-2026-10-15-s404-contract`. 263: function Loaded({ body, scope, links }: { body: FindingIndexBody; scope: FindingIndexScope; links: FindingIndexLinks }) {
264:   const firstPage = links.scope({ ...scope, cursor: null });
265:   if (body.status !== "CURRENT" || body.total_count === null) {
266:     const words = STATUS_WORDS[body.status] ?? { label: "Not known", sentence: "This read is not current." };
267:     return (
268:       <Panel heading={words.label} headingId={HEADING_ID}>
269:         <div data-finding-index-state={body.status}>
270:           <p>{words.sentence}</p>
271:           <p>No Findings are listed and no count is given. This is not a statement that the window has no Findings.</p>
272:           <Basis body={body} />
273:         </div>
274:       </Panel>
275:     );
276:   }
277:   if (body.total_count === 0) {
278:     return (
279:       <Panel heading="No Findings in this window" headingId={HEADING_ID}>
280:         <div data-finding-index-state="current-empty">
281:           <p>
282:             Every day in this window was assessed and matched for Findings through its latest selection, and no
283:             Finding overlaps the window.
284:           </p>
285:           <Basis body={body} />
286:         </div>
287:       </Panel>
288:     );
289:   }
290:   const shown = body.rows.length;
291:   return (
292:     <Panel
293:       heading={`${body.total_count} ${body.total_count === 1 ? "Finding" : "Findings"} in this window`}
294:       headingId={HEADING_ID}
295:     >
296:       <div data-finding-index-state="current-rows">
297:         <p>
298:           Newest period first. Each Finding opens at the exact version listed here, which stays the same after
299:           reload even when a newer version is recorded.
300:         </p>
301:         <Rows body={body} links={links} />

311:           ) : null}
312:         </nav>

319: function Refused({ result, scope, links }: { result: FindingIndexResult; scope: FindingIndexScope; links: FindingIndexLinks }) {
320:   const firstPage = links.scope({ ...scope, cursor: null });
321:   switch (result.status) {
322:     case "closed":
323:       return (
324:         <Panel heading="Findings list closed for this Site" headingId={HEADING_ID}>
325:           <div data-finding-index-state="closed">
326:             <p>{result.message ?? "This Site's assessments are not released."}</p>
327:             <p>
328:               No Findings are listed and no count is given. A link to an exact Finding version still opens it as
329:               audit material.
330:             </p>
331:           </div>
332:         </Panel>
333:       );
334:     case "continuity_limit":
335:       return (
336:         <Panel heading="Not known for this window" headingId={HEADING_ID}>
337:           <div data-finding-index-state="continuity-limit">
338:             <p>{result.message}</p>
339:             <p>
340:               No Findings are listed and no count is given. Choose a narrower window.
341:               {result.inspectedFrom && result.inspectedTo
342:                 ? ` Days reached: ${result.inspectedFrom} to ${result.inspectedTo}.`
343:                 : ""}
344:             </p>
345:           </div>
346:         </Panel>
347:       );
348:     case "basis_changed":
349:       return (
350:         <Panel heading="This list changed" headingId={HEADING_ID}>
351:           <div data-finding-index-state="basis-changed" data-finding-index-reason={result.reason ?? ""}>
352:             <p>The Findings for this window changed since this page was opened, so this page cannot continue.</p>
353:             <p>
354:               <Link to={firstPage} data-finding-index-restart>
355:                 Start again from the first page
356:               </Link>
357:             </p>
358:           </div>
359:         </Panel>
360:       );
361:     case "invalid":
362:       return (
363:         <Panel heading="This window cannot be shown" headingId={HEADING_ID}>

73: const SELECTION_KEYS = ["date", "finding_id", "finding_revision_id"] as const;

75: function selectionOf(params: URLSearchParams): OverviewSelection {
76:   return {
77:     date: params.get("date"),
78:     finding_id: params.get("finding_id"),
79:     finding_revision_id: params.get("finding_revision_id"),
80:   };
81: }
82: 
83: function selectionKey(selection: OverviewSelection): string {
84:   return SELECTION_KEYS.map((k) => selection[k] ?? "").join("|");
85: }
86: 
87: /** What the served day and Finding would read as in the address (the server's values, copied). */
88: function servedSelection(summary: SiteWindowSummary): OverviewSelection {
89:   return {
90:     date: summary.window.utc_date,
91:     finding_id: summary.known_finding?.id ?? null,
92:     finding_revision_id: summary.known_finding?.revision_id ?? null,
93:   };
94: }

96: export function SiteOverview({
97:   siteId,
98:   client,
99:   evidenceHref,
100:   evidencePageHref,
101:   findingHref,
102:   dispatchHref,
103:   analysisHref,
104:   windowLinks,
105: }: SiteOverviewProps) {
106:   const [result, setResult] =
107:     useState<EvidenceRead<SiteOverviewReadModel | SiteWindowSummary> | null>(null);
108:   const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
109:   const selection = selectionOf(params);
110:   const key = selectionKey(selection);
111:   // The selection the shown result already answers: writing the served day and Finding into the address must
112:   // not read the same thing again.
113:   const answered = useRef<string | null>(null);
114: 
115:   useEffect(() => {
116:     if (answered.current === `${siteId}#${key}`) {
117:       return;
118:     }
119:     let current = true;
120:     setResult(null);
121:     client.getOverview(siteId, {}, selection).then((read) => {
122:       if (!current) {
123:         return;
124:       }
125:       setResult(read);
126:       if (read.status === "loaded" && isSiteWindowSummary(read.value)) {
127:         // Date change and reload keep the served day and the selected Finding version (packet "Outcome").
128:         const served = servedSelection(read.value);
129:         const servedKey = selectionKey(served);
130:         answered.current = `${siteId}#${servedKey}`;
131:         if (servedKey !== key) {
132:           const next = new URLSearchParams(params);
133:           for (const k of SELECTION_KEYS) {
134:             const v = served[k];
135:             if (v) {
136:               next.set(k, v);
137:             } else {
138:               next.delete(k);
139:             }
140:           }
141:           setParams(next, { replace: true });
142:         }
143:       } else {
144:         answered.current = null;
145:       }
146:     });
147:     return () => {
148:       current = false;
149:     };
150:     // `params` is read through `key`, which names every parameter this read uses.
151:     // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
152:   }, [client, siteId, key]);
153: 
154:   const selectDay = (day: string) => {
155:     const next = new URLSearchParams(params);
156:     next.set("date", day);
157:     next.delete("finding_id");
158:     next.delete("finding_revision_id");
159:     setParams(next);
160:   };
161: 
162:   if (result === null) {
163:     return <p>{SITE_OVERVIEW_LOADING}</p>;
164:   }
165:   if (awaitingMigration(result)) {
166:     return <AwaitingEvidenceMigration surface="overview" />;
167:   }
168:   if (result.status !== "loaded") {
169:     return (
170:       <Panel
171:         heading="Overview unavailable"
172:         headingId="site-overview-unavailable-heading"
173:       >
174:         <p>
175:           The Overview could not be read
176:           {result.status === "unavailable" && result.message !== null
177:             ? `: ${result.message}`
178:             : "."}{" "}
179:           This is a statement about the read, not about the site&apos;s
180:           evidence.
181:         </p>
182:       </Panel>
183:     );
184:   }
185: 
186:   if (isSiteWindowSummary(result.value)) {
187:     if (windowLinks === undefined) {
188:       return (
189:         <Panel heading="Overview unavailable" headingId="site-overview-unavailable-heading">
190:           <p>This screen has no destinations for the selected-day Overview.</p>
191:         </Panel>
192:       );
193:     }
194:     return <SiteWindowOverview summary={result.value} links={windowLinks} onSelectDay={selectDay} />;
195:   }
196:   const overview = result.value;
197:   const profile = presentationProfileFor(overview.site_type);
198:   const cards = orderedOverviewCards(profile, overview.cards);
199:   const link = (signal: string, messageId: string) =>
200:     evidenceHref(signal, messageId);

101:         detail={detail}
102:         templateLineage={templateLineage}
103:         tabs={<OperatorSiteTabs siteId={siteId} current="Overview" />}
104:         parentTrail={() => [{ label: "Sites", to: sitesPath }]}
105:         overview={(canonicalId) => (
106:           <SiteOverview
107:             siteId={canonicalId}
108:             client={overview}
109:             evidencePageHref={siteEvidenceHref(canonicalId)}
110:             evidenceHref={(signal, messageId) =>
111:               siteEvidenceRecordHref(canonicalId, signal, messageId)
112:             }
113:             findingHref={(assessmentId) => findingHref(canonicalId, assessmentId)}
114:             dispatchHref={(assessmentId) =>
115:               assessmentId === undefined
116:                 ? sitePerformanceHref(canonicalId, "dispatch")
117:                 : `${sitePerformanceHref(canonicalId, "dispatch")}?interval=${encodeURIComponent(assessmentId)}`
118:             }

120:               dispatchDay: (day, revisionId) =>

122:                   revisionId ? `&revision=${encodeURIComponent(revisionId)}` : ""
123:                 }`,

127:               assumption: (revisionId) => siteFinancialAssumptionsHref(canonicalId, { revisionId }),
128:               evidenceRecord: (evidenceId) => siteEvidenceAcceptedRecordHref(canonicalId, evidenceId),
129:             }}
130:             analysisHref={(assessmentId, isFinding) =>
175:  */
176: export function findingIndexScopeQuery(scope: FindingIndexScope): string {
177:   const query = new URLSearchParams({ domain: scope.domain, from: scope.from, to: scope.to });
178:   if (scope.limit) query.set("limit", String(scope.limit));
179:   if (scope.cursor) query.set("cursor", scope.cursor);
180:   return query.toString();
181: }
182: 
183: function scopeFromQuery(params: URLSearchParams): FindingIndexScope {
184:   const today = todayWindow();
185:   const limit = Number(params.get("limit"));
186:   return {
187:     domain: params.get("domain") ?? FINDING_INDEX_DOMAIN,
188:     from: params.get("from") ?? today.from,
189:     to: params.get("to") ?? today.to,
190:     limit: params.get("limit") === null || !Number.isFinite(limit) ? null : limit,
191:     cursor: params.get("cursor"),
192:   };
193: }

196:   detail,
197:   analytics,
198:   sitesPath,
199:   findingIndex = DEFAULT_FINDING_INDEX,

204:   findingIndex?: FindingIndexClient;
205: }) {

207:   const [params] = useSearchParams();
208:   const navigate = useNavigate();
209:   const scope = scopeFromQuery(params);

221:               { label: "Sites", to: sitesPath },
222:               { label: site.site_id, to: siteDetailHref(site.site_id) },
223:               { label: "Findings" },
224:             ]}
225:           />
226:           <PageHeader
227:             title="Findings"
228:             headingId={SITE_FINDINGS_HEADING_ID}
229:             subtitle={`${site.display_name} Â· ${site.site_id}`}
230:           />
231:           <OperatorSiteTabs siteId={siteId} current="Findings" />
232:           <SiteFindingIndex
233:             siteId={site.site_id}
234:             client={findingIndex}
235:             scope={scope}
236:             links={{
237:               scope: (next) => `${siteFindingsHref(site.site_id)}?${findingIndexScopeQuery(next)}`,
238:               finding: (id, revisionId) => siteFindingHref(site.site_id, id, revisionId),
239:             }}
240:             onScope={(next) => navigate(`${siteFindingsHref(site.site_id)}?${findingIndexScopeQuery(next)}`)}

Its `match_state` checks `finding_match_pass.deferred_window_starts` before declaring a day matched (`backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_finding_store.py:407-427`); the index snapshot also carries deferred windows (`:471-480`). Do not bypass this with client-side completeness or an Overview-only query. **Backend work needed: none** for this slice; if an actual contract defect appears, return it for an Architect decision before widening scope.

+## Build contract and acceptance
+
+| Case | Overview behavior |
+| --- | --- |
+| Request | **New:** read S4-04 with the canonical Overview Site, `domain=mini-grid-dispatch`, `from=summary.window.start`, `to=summary.window.end`, first page and a bounded limit. Re-read on Site/day change; ignore or cancel an older response. The server's selected day, not the browser clock, defines the window. Do not issue the imported index request for a legacy Overview. Preserve existing Overview URL keys and the known Finding pin; an index row link opens `/sites/{site}/findings/{id}` at its `finding_revision_id`. |
+| Current, zero | Only `status=CURRENT`, `is_current=true`, `total_count=0`, rows empty and an available scoped token/watermarks may say no Findings for the selected window. Show the assessed-zero basis (including the requested day and discovered neighbour days) and a full-list destination. The S4-04 zero-row fixture is the proof, not an empty array in a mock. |
+| Pending, unassessed, stale, failed | Say Unknown/pending/stale/failed with reasons and whatever watermarks the index supplies; give no zero claim or current count. Historical pinned material remains explicitly audit-only if the read provides it, never presented as current. A deferred matched window cannot make a current empty claim. |
+| Current, positive | Show persisted canonical rows in index order with title/episode/lifecycle or claim limit, selected revision ID and exact detail link. Preserve the index selection token, count and scoped basis. If only the first page is shown, state that and link to the full Site Findings page with the same `from/to/domain`; do not imply the preview is complete. The S5-01 chosen Finding need not be the first index row if its rule differs, but same-basis discrepancies must be explained and tested. No fabricated priority, action, money or mock F-104 row. |
+| Refusal/error | Closed gate 409, continuity limit, changed basis, 401/403, invalid or unavailable read use S4-04's typed result language: no current rows/count or empty claim. Keep the Overview's other selected-day sections and its own freshness/watermark visible. A date change and reload must not resurrect an old day's list. |

If the Overview and index disagree on current basis, suppress any claim that their rows equal the Overview card choice, and offer reload. **New:** adapt/extract S4-04 presentation only as needed so the Overview shares its state vocabulary and exact-link behavior. Keep native links/buttons and visible focus order at 1440 and 390 px; Enter on a row link opens the selected revision and browser Back returns to the selected Overview day.

+# S5-02 — Finding list in Site Overview
+
+Status: implementation-ready

+## Outcome and authority
+
+In the Overview's `mini:3` Needs attention panel, show the S4-04 Finding index for the exact selected Site and half-open UTC day. Prove independently (a) scoped assessed zero rows **or** honest Unknown, and (b) persisted positive canonical rows with pinned Finding revision links. Keep S5-01's selected date, watermark, reload, known Finding pin, and keyboard/detail navigation. This list is separate from the one Finding chosen for the money card; never sum rows or infer a health status, savings, or a count from a non-current read. Demo speed takes precedence over copy polish, which belongs to the W1-conclusion task.
+
+Authority: `.ai/DECISIONS.md` `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`, `D-2026-10-15-s404-contract`, `D-2026-10-15-s501-finding-choice`; `tasks/S5-01-site-overview.md`, `tasks/S4-04-site-finding-index.md`; registry `I-S5-02`, `I-overview-finding-list-ui`, `mini:3#a/b` (`frontend/docs/architecture/v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json:243-244,316`). Do not bypass this with client-side completeness or an Overview-only query. **Backend work needed: none** for this slice; if an actual contract defect appears, return it for an Architect decision before widening scope.

+
+| Case | Overview behavior |

The S5-01 chosen Finding need not be the first index row if its rule differs, but same-basis discrepancies must be explained and tested. No fabricated priority, action, money or mock F-104 row. |
+| Refusal/error | Closed gate 409, continuity limit, changed basis, 401/403, invalid or unavailable read use S4-04's typed result language: no current rows/count or empty claim. Keep the Overview's other selected-day sections and its own freshness/watermark visible. A date change and reload must not resurrect an old day's list. |
+
+The index is its own fenced read; do not claim one atomic snapshot across the two endpoints. Display both bases so a later S3/S4 selection change is visible. If the Overview and index disagree on current basis, suppress any claim that their rows equal the Overview card choice, and offer reload. **New:** adapt/extract S4-04 presentation only as needed so the Overview shares its state vocabulary and exact-link behavior. Keep native links/buttons and visible focus order at 1440 and 390 px; Enter on a row link opens the selected revision and browser Back returns to the selected Overview day.

+# S5-02 — Finding list in Site Overview
+
+Status: implementation-ready
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true — independent slice review, then owner visual walkthrough before merge or `mini:3#a/b` promotion.
+Size: S; one build phase plus one review.
+Branch: `task/S5-02-overview-finding-list` from current main (this refinement: `plan/S5-02-refine`).
+Depends on: merged, owner-accepted S5-01 and S4-04. The open T0xx queue is not authority.
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+In the Overview's `mini:3` Needs attention panel, show the S4-04 Finding index for the exact selected Site and half-open UTC day. Prove independently (a) scoped assessed zero rows **or** honest Unknown, and (b) persisted positive canonical rows with pinned Finding revision links. Keep S5-01's selected date, watermark, reload, known Finding pin, and keyboard/detail navigation. This list is separate from the one Finding chosen for the money card; never sum rows or infer a health status, savings, or a count from a non-current read. Demo speed takes precedence over copy polish, which belongs to the W1-conclusion task.
+
+Authority: `.ai/DECISIONS.md` `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`, `D-2026-10-15-s404-contract`, `D-2026-10-15-s501-finding-choice`; `tasks/S5-01-site-overview.md`, `tasks/S4-04-site-finding-index.md`; registry `I-S5-02`, `I-overview-finding-list-ui`, `mini:3#a/b` (`frontend/docs/architecture/v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json:243-244,316`). The v6.9 `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html:410-416` and `screen-manifest.json:105-124` guide panel hierarchy and destinations; illustrative F-104 and mock money are not product evidence.

+## Verified seams (existing unless marked **new**)
+
+- `frontend/src/sites/SiteOverview.tsx:73-94,106-159,186-195` reads the imported `site-window-summary-1`, writes the served UTC day and selected Finding revision into the URL, and changes date without carrying the old pin. `frontend/src/sites/SiteWindowOverview.tsx:575-626` renders one known Finding and the `finding_list: UNAVAILABLE` message. The backend still emits that placeholder (`backend/assetops_backend/site_overview/read.py:36,213`); **new:** replace the visible placeholder in the imported-Site panel with the S4-04 client-backed list. Do not treat the Overview DTO's single `known_finding` or placeholder as an index, and do not alter its money/read contract.
+- `frontend/src/sites/findingIndexClient.ts:20-30,50-108,192-202` supplies the domain token `mini-grid-dispatch`, `FindingIndexScope`, persisted rows, exact revision IDs, token, per-day watermarks and typed result. `frontend/src/sites/SiteFindingIndex.tsx:263-315,319-420` already renders current empty/rows, non-current Unknown, closed gate and refusal states, exact links and keyboard-operable pagination. `frontend/src/shell/SiteAnalyticsFrames.tsx:175-190,195-240` owns the full Site Findings route/query. **New:** reuse the client and suitable presentational pieces inside Overview, without embedding the full index window form or second date selector; link to the full Site Findings page for pagination and deeper inspection.
+- The S4-04 read is already `GET /api/sites/{site_code}/finding-index` (`backend/assetops_backend/finding_index_api.py:3,40`). Its `match_state` checks `finding_match_pass.deferred_window_starts` before declaring a day matched (`backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_finding_store.py:407-427`); the index snapshot also carries deferred windows (`:471-480`). Do not bypass this with client-side completeness or an Overview-only query. **Backend work needed: none** for this slice; if an actual contract defect appears, return it for an Architect decision before widening scope.

+## Build contract and acceptance
+
+| Case | Overview behavior |
+| --- | --- |
+| Request | **New:** read S4-04 with the canonical Overview Site, `domain=mini-grid-dispatch`, `from=summary.window.start`, `to=summary.window.end`, first page and a bounded limit. Re-read on Site/day change; ignore or cancel an older response. The server's selected day, not the browser clock, defines the window. Do not issue the imported index request for a legacy Overview. Preserve existing Overview URL keys and the known Finding pin; an index row link opens `/sites/{site}/findings/{id}` at its `finding_revision_id`. |
+| Current, zero | Only `status=CURRENT`, `is_current=true`, `total_count=0`, rows empty and an available scoped token/watermarks may say no Findings for the selected window. Show the assessed-zero basis (including the requested day and discovered neighbour days) and a full-list destination. The S4-04 zero-row fixture is the proof, not an empty array in a mock. |
+| Pending, unassessed, stale, failed | Say Unknown/pending/stale/failed with reasons and whatever watermarks the index supplies; give no zero claim or current count. Historical pinned material remains explicitly audit-only if the read provides it, never presented as current. A deferred matched window cannot make a current empty claim. |
+| Current, positive | Show persisted canonical rows in index order with title/episode/lifecycle or claim limit, selected revision ID and exact detail link. Preserve the index selection token, count and scoped basis. If only the first page is shown, state that and link to the full Site Findings page with the same `from/to/domain`; do not imply the preview is complete. The S5-01 chosen Finding need not be the first index row if its rule differs, but same-basis discrepancies must be explained and tested. No fabricated priority, action, money or mock F-104 row. |
+| Refusal/error | Closed gate 409, continuity limit, changed basis, 401/403, invalid or unavailable read use S4-04's typed result language: no current rows/count or empty claim. Keep the Overview's other selected-day sections and its own freshness/watermark visible. A date change and reload must not resurrect an old day's list. |
+
+The index is its own fenced read; do not claim one atomic snapshot across the two endpoints. Display both bases so a later S3/S4 selection change is visible. If the Overview and index disagree on current basis, suppress any claim that their rows equal the Overview card choice, and offer reload. **New:** adapt/extract S4-04 presentation only as needed so the Overview shares its state vocabulary and exact-link behavior. Keep native links/buttons and visible focus order at 1440 and 390 px; Enter on a row link opens the selected revision and browser Back returns to the selected Overview day.

+# S5-02 — Finding list in Site Overview
+
+Status: implementation-ready
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true — independent slice review, then owner visual walkthrough before merge or `mini:3#a/b` promotion.
+Size: S; one build phase plus one review.
+Branch: `task/S5-02-overview-finding-list` from current main (this refinement: `plan/S5-02-refine`).

+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+In the Overview's `mini:3` Needs attention panel, show the S4-04 Finding index for the exact selected Site and half-open UTC day. Prove independently (a) scoped assessed zero rows **or** honest Unknown, and (b) persisted positive canonical rows with pinned Finding revision links. Keep S5-01's selected date, watermark, reload, known Finding pin, and keyboard/detail navigation. This list is separate from the one Finding chosen for the money card; never sum rows or infer a health status, savings, or a count from a non-current read. Demo speed takes precedence over copy polish, which belongs to the W1-conclusion task.
+
+Authority: `.ai/DECISIONS.md` `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`, `D-2026-10-15-s404-contract`, `D-2026-10-15-s501-finding-choice`; `tasks/S5-01-site-overview.md`, `tasks/S4-04-site-finding-index.md`; registry `I-S5-02`, `I-overview-finding-list-ui`, `mini:3#a/b` (`frontend/docs/architecture/v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json:243-244,316`). Do not treat the Overview DTO's single `known_finding` or placeholder as an index, and do not alter its money/read contract.
+- `frontend/src/sites/findingIndexClient.ts:20-30,50-108,192-202` supplies the domain token `mini-grid-dispatch`, `FindingIndexScope`, persisted rows, exact revision IDs, token, per-day watermarks and typed result. `frontend/src/sites/SiteFindingIndex.tsx:263-315,319-420` already renders current empty/rows, non-current Unknown, closed gate and refusal states, exact links and keyboard-operable pagination. `frontend/src/shell/SiteAnalyticsFrames.tsx:175-190,195-240` owns the full Site Findings route/query. **New:** reuse the client and suitable presentational pieces inside Overview, without embedding the full index window form or second date selector; link to the full Site Findings page for pagination and deeper inspection.

Its `match_state` checks `finding_match_pass.deferred_window_starts` before declaring a day matched (`backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_finding_store.py:407-427`); the index snapshot also carries deferred windows (`:471-480`). Do not bypass this with client-side completeness or an Overview-only query. **Backend work needed: none** for this slice; if an actual contract defect appears, return it for an Architect decision before widening scope.

+## Build contract and acceptance
+
+| Case | Overview behavior |
+| --- | --- |
+| Request | **New:** read S4-04 with the canonical Overview Site, `domain=mini-grid-dispatch`, `from=summary.window.start`, `to=summary.window.end`, first page and a bounded limit. Re-read on Site/day change; ignore or cancel an older response. The server's selected day, not the browser clock, defines the window. Do not issue the imported index request for a legacy Overview. Preserve existing Overview URL keys and the known Finding pin; an index row link opens `/sites/{site}/findings/{id}` at its `finding_revision_id`. |
+| Current, zero | Only `status=CURRENT`, `is_current=true`, `total_count=0`, rows empty and an available scoped token/watermarks may say no Findings for the selected window. Show the assessed-zero basis (including the requested day and discovered neighbour days) and a full-list destination. The S4-04 zero-row fixture is the proof, not an empty array in a mock. |
+| Pending, unassessed, stale, failed | Say Unknown/pending/stale/failed with reasons and whatever watermarks the index supplies; give no zero claim or current count. Historical pinned material remains explicitly audit-only if the read provides it, never presented as current. A deferred matched window cannot make a current empty claim. |
+| Current, positive | Show persisted canonical rows in index order with title/episode/lifecycle or claim limit, selected revision ID and exact detail link. Preserve the index selection token, count and scoped basis. If only the first page is shown, state that and link to the full Site Findings page with the same `from/to/domain`; do not imply the preview is complete. The S5-01 chosen Finding need not be the first index row if its rule differs, but same-basis discrepancies must be explained and tested. No fabricated priority, action, money or mock F-104 row. |
+| Refusal/error | Closed gate 409, continuity limit, changed basis, 401/403, invalid or unavailable read use S4-04's typed result language: no current rows/count or empty claim. Keep the Overview's other selected-day sections and its own freshness/watermark visible. A date change and reload must not resurrect an old day's list. |
+
+The index is its own fenced read; do not claim one atomic snapshot across the two endpoints. Display both bases so a later S3/S4 selection change is visible. If the Overview and index disagree on current basis, suppress any claim that their rows equal the Overview card choice, and offer reload. **New:** adapt/extract S4-04 presentation only as needed so the Overview shares its state vocabulary and exact-link behavior. Keep native links/buttons and visible focus order at 1440 and 390 px; Enter on a row link opens the selected revision and browser Back returns to the selected Overview day.

Historical pinned material remains explicitly audit-only if the read provides it, never presented as current. A deferred matched window cannot make a current empty claim. |
+| Current, positive | Show persisted canonical rows in index order with title/episode/lifecycle or claim limit, selected revision ID and exact detail link. Preserve the index selection token, count and scoped basis. If only the first page is shown, state that and link to the full Site Findings page with the same `from/to/domain`; do not imply the preview is complete. +| Refusal/error | Closed gate 409, continuity limit, changed basis, 401/403, invalid or unavailable read use S4-04's typed result language: no current rows/count or empty claim. Keep the Overview's other selected-day sections and its own freshness/watermark visible. A date change and reload must not resurrect an old day's list. |
+
+The index is its own fenced read; do not claim one atomic snapshot across the two endpoints. Display both bases so a later S3/S4 selection change is visible. +# S5-02 — Finding list in Site Overview
+
+Status: implementation-ready
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true — independent slice review, then owner visual walkthrough before merge or `mini:3#a/b` promotion.
+Size: S; one build phase plus one review.
+Branch: `task/S5-02-overview-finding-list` from current main (this refinement: `plan/S5-02-refine`).
+Depends on: merged, owner-accepted S5-01 and S4-04. The open T0xx queue is not authority.

+## Outcome and authority
+
+In the Overview's `mini:3` Needs attention panel, show the S4-04 Finding index for the exact selected Site and half-open UTC day. Prove independently (a) scoped assessed zero rows **or** honest Unknown, and (b) persisted positive canonical rows with pinned Finding revision links. Keep S5-01's selected date, watermark, reload, known Finding pin, and keyboard/detail navigation. This list is separate from the one Finding chosen for the money card; never sum rows or infer a health status, savings, or a count from a non-current read. Demo speed takes precedence over copy polish, which belongs to the W1-conclusion task.
+
+Authority: `.ai/DECISIONS.md` `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`, `D-2026-10-15-s404-contract`, `D-2026-10-15-s501-finding-choice`; `tasks/S5-01-site-overview.md`, `tasks/S4-04-site-finding-index.md`; registry `I-S5-02`, `I-overview-finding-list-ui`, `mini:3#a/b` (`frontend/docs/architecture/v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json:243-244,316`). +## Verified seams (existing unless marked **new**)
+
+- `frontend/src/sites/SiteOverview.tsx:73-94,106-159,186-195` reads the imported `site-window-summary-1`, writes the served UTC day and selected Finding revision into the URL, and changes date without carrying the old pin. `frontend/src/sites/SiteWindowOverview.tsx:575-626` renders one known Finding and the `finding_list: UNAVAILABLE` message. The backend still emits that placeholder (`backend/assetops_backend/site_overview/read.py:36,213`); **new:** replace the visible placeholder in the imported-Site panel with the S4-04 client-backed list. Do not treat the Overview DTO's single `known_finding` or placeholder as an index, and do not alter its money/read contract.
+- `frontend/src/sites/findingIndexClient.ts:20-30,50-108,192-202` supplies the domain token `mini-grid-dispatch`, `FindingIndexScope`, persisted rows, exact revision IDs, token, per-day watermarks and typed result. `frontend/src/sites/SiteFindingIndex.tsx:263-315,319-420` already renders current empty/rows, non-current Unknown, closed gate and refusal states, exact links and keyboard-operable pagination. `frontend/src/shell/SiteAnalyticsFrames.tsx:175-190,195-240` owns the full Site Findings route/query. **New:** reuse the client and suitable presentational pieces inside Overview, without embedding the full index window form or second date selector; link to the full Site Findings page for pagination and deeper inspection.
+- The S4-04 read is already `GET /api/sites/{site_code}/finding-index` (`backend/assetops_backend/finding_index_api.py:3,40`). Its `match_state` checks `finding_match_pass.deferred_window_starts` before declaring a day matched (`backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_finding_store.py:407-427`); the index snapshot also carries deferred windows (`:471-480`). Do not bypass this with client-side completeness or an Overview-only query. **Backend work needed: none** for this slice; if an actual contract defect appears, return it for an Architect decision before widening scope.
